# Anno 117's enums

Generated by `scripts/enums117.py` from the installed game (newest patch archive `zz_patchfiles_301.rda`); do not edit, regenerate. 604 data enums with 5276 items in 53 groups (`data/base/config/game/datasets.xml`), 7 enums only in the Lua stubs, 229 engine enums exposed to Lua.

- **Data id**: the item's `Id` in `datasets.xml`. Asset files name an item by its name; the id is what the data stores.
- **Lua**: the item's value in the generated Lua stub (`datasets.<Name>.<Item>`), an index that often differs from the data id (`Region`: `Roman` is data id 7, Lua 1). Empty: the stub lacks the item.
- **Not exported in**: the game versions whose data leaves the item out (DLC groundwork and test values).
- **Fixed by the engine**: enums the engine sizes its tables by and branches on; a mod cannot add values (`../engine-notes.md` "Engine enums cannot grow").
- **Engine enums** (`halo.*`): C++ enums the game exports to Lua; their numbers are name hashes, not indexes.

### ParticipantMessageRole

Group ParticipantMessages, dataset id 338.

E.g. for Message >Ship Killed<: "Target" = Human killed my Ship,           "Initiator" = I killed someones ship, "Observer" = VilleJorgensen killed Pirates Ship

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `Target` | 0 | 0 | I am the target of the action. Example: >Ship Killed< = Someone               killed MY ship |
| `Initiator` | 1 | 1 | I am the initiator of the action. Example: >Ship Killed< = I killed               someones ship |
| `ObserverToInitiator` | 2 | 2 | I am not directly involved in the action. Example: >Ship Killed< =               VilleJorgensen killed a Pirates ship |
| `ObserverToTarget` | 3 | 3 |  |
| `ObserverToSelf` | 4 | 4 |  |
| `InitiatorToSelf` | 5 | 5 |  |

### QuestState

Group Quest, dataset id 382.

All quest states have to be in order, from beginning of the quest to the end

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `Triggered` | 0 | 0 | quest was called out but is not doing anything yet |
| `Active` | 2 | 1 | quest is active and running. the quest is visible in the quest tracker. |
| `Reachable` | 3 | 2 | quest goals reached but the rewards have to be collected in the assignment             center |
| `Reached` | 4 | 3 | quest successfully solved |
| `Failed` | 5 | 4 | quest failed |
| `AbortedAutomatically` | 6 | 5 | quest aborted automatically |
| `AbortedManually` | 7 | 6 | quest aborted by the user |

### GoodsInRangeType

Group ConditionsAndActions, dataset id 1773.

The type of check that should be used to check if enough goods are available

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `TargetObject` | 0 | 0 | The check has to be done via goods/items on a ship |
| `Area` | 1 | 1 | The check has to be done through an area's kontor. The goods are then removed from the related kontor when available |
| `Meta` | 2 | 2 | The check is done on a meta level and meta products are subtracted when the goods are available (e.g. Money delivery) |

### ItemUsedCalculationType

Group ConditionsAndActions, dataset id 2515.

Defines the type how items are counted for the item used condition

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `AllItems` | 0 | 0 | Every item counts regardless of the location |
| `InSameArea` | 1 | 1 | Only items in the same area are counted |
| `InSameObject` | 2 | 2 | Only items in the same object are counted |

### SpawnBorderDistance

Group ConditionsAndActions, dataset id 388.

Defines the min and max distances for different spawning areas according to the         map borders. Values are percentages according to the current map's width.

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `Near` | 0 | 0 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area calculated from the map             borders (playable area). Values are percentages according to the current map's width (if             the map's width != map's length, then the longest value is taken as reference) |
| `Mid` | 1 | 1 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area calculated from the map             borders (playable area). Values are percentages according to the current map's width (if             the map's width != map's length, then the longest value is taken as reference) |
| `Far` | 2 | 2 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area calculated from the map             borders (playable area). Values are percentages according to the current map's width (if             the map's width != map's length, then the longest value is taken as reference) |

### SpawnIslandDistance

Group ConditionsAndActions, dataset id 389.

Defines the min and max distances for different spawning areas according to the         distance to an island. Values are given in tiles.

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `Near` | 0 | 0 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area to any island. Values are             absolute values in map tiles. |
| `Mid` | 1 | 1 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area to any island. Values are             absolute values in map tiles. |
| `Far` | 2 | 2 | Defines the min and max distance for spawn area to any island. Values are             absolute values in map tiles. |

### ObjectType

Group Objects, dataset id 424.

The type of any ingame object. The order of the types defines which object pool         is queried first for any request (e.g. GetObject(), DeleteObject(), ...)

| Item | Data id | Lua | Note |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| `GameObject` | 0 | 0 | default gameobject, saved into savegame, only loaded from map when loading             first time |
| `FeedbackObject` | 1 | 1 | never serialized or exported by editor |
| `NaturePreset` | 2 | 2 | editor exports every nature object into this one |
| `Nature` | 3 | 3 | nature objects created by player |
| `EditorObject` | 4 | 4 | always serialized from map, never stored to savegames |
| `EditorOnlyObject` | 7 | 5 |  |
| `NotSerialized` | 5 | 6 | Objects that are not serialized by GameObjectManager. Assumption: Not             relevant for game logic |
| `Prop` | 6 | 7 | only a visual doodad, never saved |
